# Master Index — General Research

Every source in this repo, what tier it is, how much of it we can actually use,
and which pipeline table/cell it backs or could replace. `sources.csv` holds the
same data machine-readable (one row per item, with domain dir); each domain also
keeps its own `<domain>/FINDINGS.md` with the prose and per-number comparison
tables.

Legend for **access** (the first word of each `access_status`; README "Access classes" has all seven):
- `full_text_hosted` — full PDF committed under `<domain>/full_texts/`, legally redistributable (CC-licensed arXiv preprints, NASA public-domain docs, CC-BY articles); the file and its licence are listed in `full_texts_manifest.csv`.
- `open_not_pulled` — open access but not downloadable from this machine (bot-block) or license needs a user decision; recorded with URL + reason.
- `verified_live_not_pulled`, `open_service`, `public_domain_excerpt_hosted`, `skipped` — read live but not committed; a live database; a public-domain excerpt quoted in FINDINGS; deliberately not hosted.
- `registered_not_pulled` — an upstream citation registered in Round 74 so the dependency is visible: DOI checked against Crossref or landing page checked live, full text not sought, nothing extracted yet.

Tiers are T1 (peer-reviewed), T2 (agency technical reports, AIAA/IAC), T3 (authoritative government or dataset publications) and, since Round 74, T4 (grey sources the upstream rows cite: company documents, news, encyclopedias, vendor posts, textbooks, software).

Findings that contradict an upstream cell are tracked, with their current status, in `revision_candidates.csv`.

Sources the owner has rejected are listed, with the reason, under "Rejected sources" above the research log, and nowhere else.

## Domain 1 — Density by spectral type, PGM factors, population statistics

_Backings: per-spectral-type density and PGM factors in AsteroidCatalog `reference/taxonomy_composition.csv` (loaded by economicspace `catalog.py`); ranking quality of the whole pipeline._

S/X/Cgh/D agree; B/L/K sit ~0.7–1 g/cm³ below our values (revision candidates) |

## Domain 2 — Composition → commodity value mapping

_Backings: `mineral_value.py` in-pipeline mineralogy, destination pricing._

| id | tier | source (short) | access | backs / could replace |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| nat_dms2023 | T1 | DES metal extraction from asteroid proxies (Sci Rep 13:6497, CC-BY-4.0) | full text hosted | **85% surface metal / 15% silicate** for Psyche-like NEAs vs our M-row `metal_fraction=0.50` — the biggest value-side discrepancy found in round 1; also first lab proof of space-feasible beneficiation chemistry |
| asime2018 | T2 | Graps et al., ASIME white paper on asteroid composition (arXiv:1904.11831) | read live, not hosted (un-hosted 2026-09-26: arXiv default licence); numbers in extracted_data | reliability bounds for the spectral-type→composition step: water up to 22% in meteorites; hydration correlates with albedo <7.5% — a guardrail our pipeline lacks |

| cannon2023 | T1 | Cannon, Gialich & Acain, "Precious and structural metals on asteroids" (PSS 215:105608) | **skipped — user decision 2026-09-17**: OA but CC-BY-NC-ND; not committed to a public repo. Companion to bennu_volatiles_2025 |

Northrop Grumman LunA-10 Lunar Rail Network Infrastructure Study NG24-0751 (T4 grey; Distribution Statement A 'Approved for Public Release' hosted): projected annual market value $1.6B-$8B = 500-2,500 MT/yr under an explicit Starship price ladder ($300-600/kg LEO -> ~$1,400-2,600/kg GEO -> ~$2,000-3,800/kg Moon).
